8. tā kahaṃ te vaddho'vaddhī muhuttāṇaṃ āhie tti, vadejjā ?
tā aṭṭha egūṇavīse muhuttasae sattāvīsaṃ ca saṭṭibhāge muhuttassa āhie ti vadejjā | 1 savvasūramaṃḍalamagge sūrassa gamaṇāgamaṇa-rāiṃdiyappamāṇaṃ
(ka) muhūrto kī hāni - vṛddhi kā yaha sūtra yahāṃ kaise diyā gayā hai ? yaha eka vicāraṇīya praśna hai |
sūryaprajñapti ke prārambha meṃ utthānikā ke bāda bīsa prābhṛtoṃ ke prāthamika viṣayoṃ kī prarūpaka pāṃca gāthāe~ haiṃ | unameṃ se prathama gāthā meṃ prathama prābhṛta ke prathama prābhṛtaprābhṛta kī prāthamika viṣayasūcaka gāthā kā 'kai maṃḍalāī vaccai' yaha prathama pada hai| isake anusāra 'eka varṣa meṃ sūrya kitane maṃḍaloṃ meṃ eka vāra aura kitane maṃḍaloṃ meṃ do vāra gati karatā hai|' yaha viṣaya hai | vṛttikāra śrīmalayagiri ukta pada kī vyākhyā isa prakāra karate haiṃ- 'prathame prābhṛte - sūryo varṣamadhye kati maṇḍalānyekavāraṃ, kati vā maṇḍalāni dviḥ kṛtvā vrajatītyetannirūpaṇīyam | kimuktaṃ bhavati ? evaṃ gautamena praśne kṛte tadanantaraṃ sarvaṃ tadviṣayaṃ nirvacanaṃ prathame prābhṛte vaktavyamiti |' kintu prathama prābhṛta ke āṭha prābhṛtaprābhṛtoṃ kī viṣayaprarūpaka do gāthāoṃ meṃ se prathama gāthā ke prathama pada meṃ 'vaḍḍho'vaḍḍhī muhuttāṇaṃ' yaha pada hai| isake anusāra prathama prābhṛta ke prathama prābhṛtaprābhṛta meṃ prathama sūtra meṃ vṛttikāra ke anusāra cāra prakāra ke māsoṃ ke muhūrtī kī hāni - vṛddhi kā prarūpaṇa hai |
vṛttikāra śrīmalayagiri ukta pada kī vyākhyā isa prakāra karate haiṃ- 'prathamasya prābhṛtasya satke prathame prābhṛtaprābhṛte muhūrtānāṃ divasa - rātrigatānāṃ vṛdvyapavṛddhī vaktavye |'
viṣayaprarūpaka saṃgrahaṇī gāthāoṃ kī racanā ke pūrva evaṃ vṛttikāra ke pūrva yaha vyutkrama ho gayā hai|
vṛttikāra svayaṃ ukta vyutkrama kī upekṣā kara gae to anya sāmānya śrutadharoṃ kā to kahanā hī kyā ? yaha sūtra kramānusāra kahāṃ honā cāhiye, isa saṃbaṃdha meṃ āge yathāsthāna likhane kā saṃkalpa hai|
(kha) muhūrtoṃ kī hāni - vṛddhi kā yaha sūtra bhī khaṇḍita pratīta hotā hai, kyoṃki prastuta sūtra ke praśnasūtra meṃ muhūrtoṃ kī hāni - vṛddhi kā praśna hai, kintu uttarasūtra meṃ kevala nakṣatramāsoṃ ke muhūrtoṃ kā hī kathana hai |

9. tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabbhaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo savvabāhiraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, savvabāhirāo maṃḍalāo savvabbhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, esa ṇaṃ addhā kevaiyaṃ rāiṃdiyagge ṇaṃ āhite tti vadejā? tā tiṇṇi chāvaṭhe rāiṃdiyasae rāiṃdiyagge ṇaṃ āhite tti vadejjā| sūramaṃḍalesu sūrassa saī dukkhutto vā cāraṃ .

paḍhame chammāse vā docce chammāse vā ṇatthi paṇṇarasamuhutte divase bhavai, ṇatthi paṇṇarasamuhuttā rāī bhavai|
tattha ṇaṃ kaṃ heuṃ vadejjā?

duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai dohiṃ egasaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahiyā, se nikkhamamāṇe sūrie doccaṃsi ahorattaṃsi abbhittara taccaṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai|
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie abbhitaraṃ taccaṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, cauhiṃ egasaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇe,

evaṃ khalu eeṇaṃ uvāeṇaṃ ṇikkhamamāṇe sūrie tayāṇaṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalāo tayāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe do do egaṭṭhibhāgamuhutte egamege maṃḍale divasakhettassa ṇivuḍḍhemāṇe ṇivuḍḍhemāṇe rayaṇikhettassa abhivuḍḍhemāṇe abhivuḍḍhemāṇe savvabāhiramaṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai,
.. tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabbhaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo savvabāhiraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai tayā ṇaṃ savvabbhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ paṇihāya ege ṇaṃ tesīe ṇaṃ rāiṃdiyasae ṇaṃ tiṇṇi chāvaḍhe egasaṭṭi bhāgamuhuttasae divasa-khettassa ṇivuḍḍhittā rayaṇi-khettassa abhivuḍḍhittā cāraṃ carai tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapattā ukkosiyoṃ aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, jahaṇṇae bārasamuhutte divase bhavai,
esa ṇaṃ paḍhame chammāse, esa ṇaṃ paḍhamassa chamāsassa pajjavasāṇe| se pavisamāṇe sūrie doccaṃ chammāsaṃ ayamāṇe paḍhamaṃsi ahorattaṃsi bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai,
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, dohiṃ egasaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ uṇā, duvālasamuhutte divase bhavaī, dohi egasaṭṭhibhāgamuhuteti āhie,
se pavisamāṇe sūrie bāhiraṃ taccaṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai||

duvālasamuhutte divase bhavai, cauhiṃ egasaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahie,
evaṃ khalu eeṇaṃ uvāeṇaṃ pavisamāṇe sūrie tayāṇaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo tayāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe do do egasaṭṭhibhāgamuhutte egamege maṃḍale rayaṇikhessa ṇivuḍḍhemāṇe ṇivuḍḍhemāṇe divasakhettassa abhivaḍḍhemāṇe abhivaḍḍhemāṇe savvabbhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai,

12. tā kahaṃ te addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiī āhitāti vadejā ? tattha khalu ime duve addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiī paṇṇattā, taṃ jahā - 1. dāhiṇā ceva addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiī, 2. uttarā ceva addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiī| tā kahaṃ te dāhiṇā addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiī āhitāti vadejā ?
tā ayaṇṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve savvadīva-samudāṇaṃ savvabhaṃtarāe savvakhuḍḍāge vaṭṭe jāva joyaṇasayasahassamāyāma - vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ tiṇṇi joyaṇasayasahassāī, donni ya sattāvīse joyaṇasae, tiṇṇi kose aṭṭhāvīsaṃ ca dhaṇusayaṃ terasa ya aṃgulāī addhaṃgulaṃ ca kiṃcivisesāhie parikkheveṇaṃ paṇṇatte|
___mā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvanbhaṃtaraṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiti uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapatte ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, jahaṇiyā duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai| ___ . se nikkhamamāṇe sūrie ṇavaṃ saṃvaccharaṃ ayamāṇe paḍhamaṃsi ahorattaṃsi dāhiṇāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassādipadesāe abbhiṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalaṃ saṃṭhiī uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai|
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie abhiṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiī uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhuttehiṃ divase bhavai dohiṃ egaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇe|
duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahiyā|
se nikkhamamāṇe sūrie doccaṃsi ahorattaṃsi uttarāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassādipadesāe abbhiṃtaraṃ taccaṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhitiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai|

evaṃ khalu eeṇaṃ uvāeṇaṃ ṇikkhamamāṇe sūrie tayāṇaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo tayāṇaṃtaramaṃḍalassa taṃsi taṃsi desaṃsi taṃ taṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhitiṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe dāhiṇāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassādipadesāe savvabāhiraṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhitiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़ |
10
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabāhiraṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhitiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़, tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapattā ukkosiyā aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, jahaṇṇae duvālasamuhutte divase bhavai |
esa ṇaṃ paḍhame chammāse, esa ṇaṃ paḍhamassa chammāssa pajjavasāṇe |
se pavisamāṇe sūrie doccaṃ chammāsaṃ ayamāṇe paḍhamaṃsi ahorattaṃsi uttarāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassādipadesāe bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhitiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़ tā jayā ṇaṃ sūriye bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ dāhiṇaaddhamaṃḍalasaṭhitiṃ uvasaṃkamitā cāraṃ caraḍa़, tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇā,
duvālasamutte divase bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahie,
se pavisamāṇe sūrie doccaṃsi ahorattaṃsi dāhiṇāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassādipadesāe bāhiraṃtaraṃ taccaṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhitiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़,
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie bāhiraṃ taccaṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhitiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़, tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, cauhiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇā, duvālasamuhute divase bhavai, cauhiṃ egāṭṭhibhāgamuhutehiṃ ahie,
evaṃ khalu eeṇaṃ uvāeṇaṃ pavisamāṇe sūrie tayāṇaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo tayāṇaṃtaraṃsi taṃsi taṃsi desaṃsi taṃ taṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiiṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe uttarāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassādipadesāe savvantaraṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhi uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai |
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabdhaṃtaraṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhitiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapatte ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, jahaṇṇiyā duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai |
esa ṇaṃ docce chammāse, esa ṇaṃ doccassa chammāsassa pajjavasāṇe,
esa ṇaṃ āicce saṃvacchare, esa ṇaṃ āiccassa saṃvaccharassa pajjavasāṇe | sūrassa uttarāaddhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiī
13. tā kahaṃ te uttarā addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiī āhiteti vadejjā ?
tā ayaṇṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve savvadīva-samuddāṇaṃ savvabdhaṃtarāe savvakhuḍ ḍāge vaṭṭe jāva joyaṇasayasahassamāyāma-vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ, tiṇṇi joyaṇasayasahassāiṃ, donni ya sattāvīse joyaṇasae, tiṇṇi

prathama prābhṛta- dvitīya prābhṛtaprābhṛta ]
[ 11
kose aṭṭhāvīsaṃ ca dhaṇusayaṃ terasa ya aṃgulāī, arddhagulaṃ ca kiṃci visesāhie parikkheveṇaṃ paṇṇatte, tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabdhaṃtaraṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़ tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapatte ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, jahaṇṇiyā duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai,
se nikkhamamāṇe sūrie ṇavaṃ saṃvaccharaṃ ayamāṇe paḍhamaṃsi ahorattaṃsi uttarāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassāipaesāe abbhaṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraī |
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie abdhaṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhi uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़, tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇe,
duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahiyā |
seṇikkhamamāṇe sūrie doccaṃsi ahorattaṃsi dāhiṇāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassāipaesāe abbhiṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ taccaṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhi uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़ |
tājā sūri abdhiṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ taccaṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़ tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, cauhiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇe,
duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, cauhiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahiyā,
evaṃ khalu eeṇaṃ uvāeṇaṃ ṇikkhamamāṇe sūrie tayāṇaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo tayāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe taṃsi taṃsi desaṃsi taṃ taṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiī saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe uttarāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassāipaesāe savvabāhiraṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhi uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़,
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabāhiraṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़, tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapattā ukkosiyā aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāi bhavai, jahaṇṇae duvālasamuhutte divase bhavai,
esa ṇaṃ paḍhame chammāse, esa ṇaṃ paḍhamassa chammāsassa pajjavasāṇe,
se pavisamāṇe sūrie doccaṃ chammāsaṃ ayamāṇe paḍhamaṃsi ahorattaṃsi dāhiṇāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassāipaesāe bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhi uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़,
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ uttaraṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiṅgaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़ tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūgā, duvālasamuhute divase bhavai dohiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhutehiṃ ahie,
se pavisamāṇe sūrie doccaṃsi ahorattaṃsi uttarāe aṃtarāe bhāgāe tassāipaesāe bāhiraṃ tacchaṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़,
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie bāhiraṃ taccaṃ dāhiṇaṃ addhamaṃḍalasaṃṭhiiṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़ tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai cauhiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇā,

14. tā kiṃ te ciṇṇaṃ paḍicarati āhiteti vadejjā ?
tattha khalu ime duve sūriyā paṇṇattā, taṃ jahā bhārahe ceva sūrie | eravae ceva sūrie |
tāṇaṃ duve sūriyā patteyaṃ patteyaṃ
tīsāe tīsāe muhuttehiṃ egamegaṃ addhamaṃḍalaṃ caraḍa़,
saṭṭī saṭṭhī muhuttehiṃ egamegaṃ maṃḍalaṃ saṃghātayati |
tā nikkhamamāṇe khalu ete duve sūriyā ṇo aṇṇamaṇṇassa ciṇṇaṃ paḍicaraṃti,
pavisamāṇā khalu ete duve sūriyā aṇṇamaṇṇassa ciṇṇaṃ paḍicaraṃti taṃ sayamegaṃ coyālaṃ, pa. - tattha ṇaṃ ko hetu, tti vadejjā ?
u. - tā ayaṇṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve savvadīva-samuddāṇaṃ savvabdhaṃtarāe savvakhuḍḍāge vaṭṭe jāva joyaṇasayasahassamāyāma- vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ, tiṇṇi joyaṇasayasahassāī, donni ya sattāvīse joyaṇasae, tiṇṇi kose aṭṭhāvīsaṃ ca dhaṇusayaṃ, terasa ya aṃgulāī, arddhagulaṃ ca kiciṃ visesāhie parikkheveṇaṃ paṇṇatte |
-
-
tattha ṇaṃ ayaṃ bhārahae ceva sūrie jaṃbuddīvassa dīvassa pāīṇapaḍīṇāyayāe udīṇa- dāhiṇāyayāe jīvāe maṃḍalaṃ cauvīsaeṇaṃ saeṇaṃ chettā - dāhiṇa-puratthimillaṃsi caubbhāgamaṃḍalaṃsi bāṇautiya sūriyamayāiṃ jāī sūrie appaṇā ceva ciṇṇāī paḍicarai,
tattha ṇaṃ ayaṃ bhārahe sūrie eravayassa sūriyassa jaṃbuddīvassa dīvassa pāīṇa-paḍīṇāyayāe udīṇadāhīṇāyayāe jīvāe maṃḍalaṃ cauvīsaeṇaṃ saraṇaṃ chettā uttara-puratthimillaṃsi caubbhāgamaṃḍalaṃsi bāṇauḍa़ya sūriyamayāiṃ jāī sūrie parassa ceva ciṇṇāī paḍicarai,
dāhiṇa-paccatthimillaṃsi caubbhāgamaṃḍalaṃsi ekkāṇauiyaṃ sūriyamayāī jāī sūrie parassa ceva ciṇṇāī paḍicarai,

prathama prābhṛta
[caturtha prābhṛtaprābhṛta] sūriyāṇaṃ aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtara-cāraṃ
15. tā kevaiyaṃ ee duve sūriyā aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭu cāraṃ caraṃti, āhiteti vadejjā? tattha khalu imāo cha paḍivattīo paṇṇattāo, taṃ jahā - 1. tattha ege evamāhaṃsu -
tā egaṃ joyaṇasahassaṃ egaṃ ca tettīsaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭu sūriyā cāraṃ caraṃti, āhiteti vadejā, ege evamāhaṃsu|
2. ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu -
tā egaṃ joyaṇasahassaṃ egaṃ ca cottīsaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭu sūriyā cāraṃ caraṃti, āhiteti vadejā, ege evamāhaṃsu,
3. ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu -
tā egaṃ joyaṇasahassaṃ egaṃ ca paṇatīsaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ. kaṭu sūriyā cāraṃ caraṃti, āhiteti vadejjā, ege evamāhaṃsu,
4-1. ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu -
tā egaṃ dīvaṃ, egaṃ samudaṃ aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭu sūriyā cāraṃ caraṃti, āhiteti vadejā, ege evamāhaṃsu,
5-2. ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu -
tā do dīve, do samudde aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭu sūriyā cāraṃ caraṃti, āhiteti vadejā, ege evamāhaṃsu,
6-3. ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu -
tā tiṇṇi dīve, tiṇi samudde, aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭu sūriyā cāraṃ caraṃti, āhiteti vadejā, ege evamāhaṃsu,

﻿________________

16 ]
[ sūryaprajñaptisūtra
vayaṃ puṇa evaṃ vayāmo -
tā paṃca paṃca joyaṇāiṃ paṇatīsaṃ ca egaṭṭhibhāge joyaṇassa egamege maṃḍale aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ abhivaḍḍhemāṇā vā, nivaḍḍhemāṇā vā sūriyā cāraṃ caraṃti, āhiteti vadejā,
tattha ṇaṃ ko heu ? āhiteti vadejā, __tā ayaṃ ṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve savvadīva-samuddāṇaṃ savvabbhaṃtarāe savvakhuḍḍāge vaṭṭe jāva joyaṇasayasahassamāyāma-vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ, tiṇṇi joyaṇasayasahassāī doṇṇi ya sattāvīse joyaṇasae, tiṇṇi kose aṭṭhāvīsaṃ ca dhaṇusayaṃ terasaya-aṃgulāī addhaṃgulalaṃ ca kiṃci visesāhie parikkheveṇaṃ paṇṇatte,
1. tā jayā ṇaṃ ete duve sūriyā savvabbhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti,
tayā ṇaṃ ṇavaṇauī joyaṇasahassāiṃ, chacca cattāle joyaṇasae aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭṭu cāraṃ caraṃti āhiteti vadejā,
tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapatte ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai jahaṇiyā duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai,
2. te nikkhamamāṇā sūriyā ṇavaṃ saṃvaccharaṃ ayamāṇā paḍhamaṃsi ahorattaṃsi anbhiṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti,
tā jayā ṇaṃ ete duve sūriyā abbhiṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti,
tayā ṇaṃ ṇavaṇauī joyaṇasahassāī chacca paṇayāle joyaṇasae paṇatīsaṃ ca egaṭṭhibhāge joyaṇassa aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭu cāraṃ caraṃti āhiteti vadejā,
tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭhibhāga muhuttehiṃ ūṇe, duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai dohiṃ egaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahiyā, 3. te nikkhamamāṇā sūriyā doccaṃsi ahorattaṃsi abbhiṃtaraṃ taccaṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti, tā jayā ṇaṃ ete duve sūriyā abbhiṃtaraṃ taccaṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti,
tayā ṇaṃ ṇavaṇauī joyaṇasahassāī chacca ikkāvaṇṇe joyaṇasae nava ya egaṭṭhibhāge joyaṇassa aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭu cāra caraṃti āhiteti vadejā,
tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai cauhiṃ egaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇe, duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai cauhiṃ egaṭṭhibhāgamuhutehiṃ ahiyā,
evaṃ khalu eeṇaṃ uvāeṇaṃ nikkhamamāṇā ete duve sūriyā tayāṇaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo tayāṇataraṃ maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇā saṃkamamāṇā paṃca paṃca joyaṇāī paṇatīsaṃ ca egaṭṭhibhāge joyaṇassa egamege maṃḍale aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ abhivaḍḍhe māṇā abhivaḍḍhe māṇā, savvabāhiraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā caraṃ caraṃti,

prathama prābhṛta - caturtha prābhṛtaprābhṛta ]
bhavai,
[ 17
tā jayā ṇaṃ ete duve sūriyā savvabāhiraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti,
tāṇaṃ egaṃ joyaṇasayasahassaṃ chacca saṭṭhe joyaṇasae aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭṭu cāraṃ caraṃti, tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapattā ukkosiyā aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai jahaṇṇae duvālasamuhutte divase
esa ṇaṃ paḍhame chammāse, esa ṇaṃ paḍhamassa chammāsassa pajjavasāṇe,
2. te pavisamāṇā sūriyā doccaṃchammāsaṃ ayamāṇāṃ paḍhamaṃsi ahorattaṃsi bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti,
tā jayā ṇaṃ ete duve sūriyā bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃmaṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti,
tayā ṇaṃ egaṃ joyaṇasayasahassaṃ chacca cauppaṇṇe joyaṇasae chattīsaṃ ca egadvibhāge joyaṇassa, aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭṭū cāraṃ caraṃti,
tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇā,
duvālasamuhutte divase bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahie,
3. te pavisamāṇā sūriyā doccaṃsi ahorattaṃsi bāhiraṃ taccaṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti, tā jayā ṇaṃ ete duve sūriyā bāhiraṃ tacchaṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti,
tāṇaṃ egaṃ joyaṇasayasahassaṃ chacca aḍayāle joyaṇasae bāvaṇṇaṃ ca egaṭṭibhāge joyaṇassa, aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭṭū cāraṃ caraṃti,
bhavai,
tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, cauhiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇā,
duvālasamuhutte divase bhavai, cauhiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahie,
evaṃ khalu eeṇaṃ uvāeṇaṃ pavisamāṇā ete duve sūriyā tayāṇaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo tayāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇā saṃkamamāṇā paṃca paṃca joyaṇāī paṇatīse ca egaṭṭibhāge joyaṇassa egamege maṃḍale aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ nivaḍḍhemāṇā nivaḍḍhemāṇā savvabdhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti,
tā jayā ṇaṃ duve sūriyā savvabdhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍala uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraṃti,
tayā ṇaṃ ṇavaṇauiṃ joyaṇasahassāiṃ chacca cattāle joyaṇasae aṇṇamaṇṇassa aṃtaraṃ kaṭṭū cāraṃ
caraṃti,
tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapatte, ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, jahaṇṇiyā duvālasamuhuttā rāī
esa ṇaṃ docce chammāse esa ṇaṃ doccassa chammāsassa pajjavasāṇe,
esa ṇaṃ āicce saṃvacchare, esa ṇaṃ āiccassa saṃvaccharassa pajjavasāṇe |

prathama prābhṛta
[chaṭhā prābhṛtaprābhṛta] sūrassa egamege rāidie maṃḍalāo maṃḍalasaṃkamaṇakhettacāraṃ
18. tā kevaiyaṃ te egamege ṇaṃ rāidie ṇaṃ vikaṃpaittā vikaṃpaittā sūrie cāra carai, āhiteti vadejā?

ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu -
5. tā adhuṭṭhāiṃ joyaṇāī egamegeṇaṃ, rāidieṇaṃ vikaṃpaittā vikaṃpaittā sūrie cāraṃ carai, ege evamāhaṃsu,
ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu -
7. tā cattārijoyaṇāiṃ addhabāvaṇṇaṃ ca tesīisayabhāge joyaṇassa egamegeṇaṃ, rāidieṇaṃ vikaṃpaittā vikaṃpaittā sūrie cāraṃ carai, ege evamāhaṃsu,

prathama prābhṛta
[ aṣṭama prābhṛtaprābhṛta ]
sūrassa savvamaṃḍalāṇaṃ bāhallaṃ āyāma-vikkhabhaṃ parikkhevaṃ ca
20. tā savvā vi ṇaṃ maṃḍalavayā -
ke vaiyaṃ bāhalleṇaṃ ?
kevaiyaṃ āyāma - vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ ?
kevaiyaṃ parikkheveṇaṃ ? āhiteti vadejjā |
tattha khalu imā tiṇṇi paḍivattīo paṇṇattāo, taṃ jahā
tatthege evamāhaṃsu
1 - tā savvā vi ṇaṃ maṃḍalavayā joyaṇaṃ bāhalleṇaṃ,
egaṃ joyaṇasahassaṃ egaṃ tettīsaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ āyāma - vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ,
tiṇi joyaṇasahassāiṃ tiṇṇi ya ṇavaṇauī joyaṇasae parikkheveṇaṃ paṇṇattā, ege evamāhaṃsu,
-
ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu
2 - tā savvā viṇaṃ maṃḍalavayā joyaṇaṃ bāhalleṇaṃ,
-
egaṃ joyaṇasahassaṃ egaṃ ca cauttīsaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ āyāma - vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ,
tiṇṇi joyaṇasahassāī castāri viuttarāī joyaṇasayāiṃ parikkheveṇaṃ paṇṇattā, ege evamāhaṃsu, ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu -
3- tā savvā vi ṇaṃ maṃḍalavayā joyaṇaṃ bāhalleṇaṃ,
egaṃ joyaṇasahassaṃ egaṃ ca paṇatīsaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ āyāma - vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ,

vayaṃ puṇa evaṃ vayāmo

[ sūryaprajñaptisūtra
aṇiyayā āyāma-vikkhaṃbha-parikkheveṇaṃ, āhiteti vadejā, tattha ṇaṃ ko heū ? tti vadejā|
tā ayaṃ ṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve savvadīva-samudāṇaṃ savvabbhaṃtarāe savvakhuḍḍāge vaṭṭe jāva joyaṇasahassamāyāma-vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ, tiṇi joyaṇasayasahassāiṃ, doṇṇi ya sattāvīse joyaṇasae, tiṇṇi kose, aṭṭhāvīsaṃ ca dhaṇusayaṃ, terasa ya aṃgulāī, addhaṃgulaṃ ca kiṃci visesāhie parikkheveṇaṃ paṇṇatte,
1. tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabbhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ sā maṃḍalavayā aḍayālīsaṃ egaṭṭhibhāge poyaṇassa bāhelleṇaṃ, ṇavaṇaui joyaṇasahassāī chacca cattāle joyaṇasayāī āyāma-vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ,
tiṇṇi joyaṇasayasahassāiṃ paṇṇarasa joyaṇasahassāiṃ egūṇaṇauī joyaṇāī kiṃci visesāhie parikkheveṇaṃ,
tayāṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapatte ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, jahaṇiyā duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai|
2. se nikkhammamāṇe sūrie ṇavaṃ saṃvaccharaṃ ayamāṇe paḍhamaṃsi ahorattaṃsi abbhitarāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai,
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie abhiṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ sā maṃḍalavayā aḍayālīsaṃ egaṭṭhibhāge joyaṇassa bāhalleṇaṃ, .
ṇavaṇauī joyaṇasahassāiṃ chacca paṇayāle joyaṇasae paṇatīsaṃ ca egaṭṭhibhāge joyaṇassa āyāmavikkhaṃbheṇaṃ,
tiṇṇi joyaṇasayasahassāiṃ paṇṇarasa joyaṇasahassāī egaṃ cauttaraṃ joyaṇasayaṃ kiṃci visesūṇaṃ
1. sūryaprajñapti tathā jambūdvīpraprajñapti ke sūtroṃ meṃ sūryamaṇḍala kā āyāma-viṣkambha kahā gayā hai kintu samavāyāṃga sūtra meṃ kevala viṣkambha hī kahā gayā hai| isakā samādhāna yaha hai ki vṛttākāra kā āyāma-viṣkambha sadā samāna hotā hai, sūryamaṇḍala vṛttākāra hai, ataḥ kevala viṣkambha kahane se āyāma aura viṣkambha donoṃ samajha lene cāhiye|
sūryaprajñapti meṃ sūryamaṇḍala kā bāhalya eka yojana ke ikasaṭha bhāgoṃ meṃ se aḍa़tālīsa bhāga jitanā kahā gayā hai| jambūdvīpaprajñapti meṃ sūryamaṇḍala kā bāhalya eka yojana ke ikasaṭha bhāgoṃ meṃ se caubīsa bhāga jitanā kahā gayā hai| ina do prakāra ke bāhalya pramāṇo meṃ se kauna sā vāstavika hai, yaha śodha kā viṣaya hai| sūryaprajñapti meṃ sūryamaṇḍala kā āyāma-viṣkambha aura paridhi bāhyābhyantara maṇḍaloṃ kī apekṣā aniyata hai, aisā likhā hai kintu jambūdvīpaprajñapti meṃ sūryamaṇḍala kā āyāma, viṣkambha aura paridhi aniyata nahīṃ likhī hai|
jambūdvīpaprajñapti meṃ sūryamaṇḍala kā āyāma-viṣkambha aura paridhi jo kahī hai vaha ābhyantara yā bāhyamaṇḍaloṃ kī hai ? kyoṃki sūryaprajñapti meṃ kathita bāhyābhyantara maṇḍaloṃ ke āyāma-viṣkambhapramāṇoṃ meṃ jambūdvīpaprajñaptikathita āyāma-viṣkambha paridhi kā pramāṇa milatā nahīṃ hai|

parikkheveṇaṃ,

egaṃ joyaṇasayasahassaṃ chacca cauppaṇe joyaṇasae chavvīsaṃ ca egaṭṭhibhāge joyaṇassa āyāmavikkhaṃbheṇaṃ,

[ 29
savvā vi ṇaṃ maṃḍalaṃtariyā do joyaṇāiṃ vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ,
esa ṇaṃ addhā tesīya sayapaḍuppaṇṇe paṃcadasuttare joyaṇasae āhie tti vaejā,
tā abdhiṃtarāo maṃḍalavayāo bāhiraṃ maṃḍalavayaṃ bāhirāo vā maṃḍalavayāo abdhiṃtaraṃ maṃḍalavayaṃ, esaṇaṃ addhā ke vaiyaṃ āhie tti vadejjā ?
tā paṃcadasuttare joyaṇasae āhie tti vaejjā,
abdhiṃtarāe maṃḍalavayāe bāhirā maṃḍalavayā esa ṇaṃ addhā kevaiyaṃ āhie ti vaejjā ?
tā paṃcadasuttare joyaṇasae aḍayālīsaṃ ca egadvibhāge joyaṇassa ahiyā,
tā abdhiṃtarāo maṃḍalavayāo bāhiramaṃḍalavayā bāhirāo maṃḍalavayāo abdhiṃtaramaṃḍalavayā - esa. ṇaṃ addhā ke vaiyaṃ āhie ti vadejjā ?
tā paṃcanavuttare joyaṇasae terasa egadvibhāge joyaṇassa āhie tti vadejjā,
abbhiṃtarāo maṃḍalavayāo bāhirā maṃḍalavayā, bāhirāe maṃḍalavayāe abdhiṃtara-maṃḍalavayā esa ṇaṃ addhā kevaiyā āhie tti vadejjā ?
tā paṃcadasuttare joyaṇasae āhie ti vadejjā |

dvitīya prābhṛta
[dvitīya prābhṛtaprābhṛta] sūrassa maṃḍalāo maṃḍalāṃtara-saṃkamaṇaṃ
22. tā kahaṃ te maṃḍalāo maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe sūrie cāraṃ carai āhie ? ti vaejā, tattha khalu imāo duve paḍivattīo paṇṇatāo taṃ jahā - tatthege evamāhaṃsu1. tā maṃḍalāo maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe sūrie bheyadhāeṇaṃ saṃkāmai, ege evamāhaṃsa, ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu - 2. tā maṃḍalāo maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe sūrie kaṇṇakalaṃ nivveḍhei, ege evamāhaṃsu, . tattha ṇaṃ je te evamāhaṃsu - 1. tā maṃḍalāo maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe sūrie bheyadhāeṇaṃ' saṃkāmai, tesiṇaṃ ayaṃ dose,
"tā jeṇaṃtareṇaṃ maṃḍalāo maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe bheyadhāeṇaṃ saṃkamai-evaiyaṃ ca ṇaṃ addhaṃ purao na gacchai, purao purao agacchamāṇe maṃḍalakālaṃ parihavei' tesi ṇaṃ ayaṃ dose|
tattha ṇaṃ je te evamāhaṃsu -
1. maṇḍalādaparamaṇḍalaṃ saṃkrāman saṃkramitumicchan sūryo bhedaghātena saṃkramati, bhedo maṇḍalasya maṇḍalasyāpāntarālaṃ tatra ghāto - gamanaṃ, etacca prāgevoktaṃ, tena saṃkrāmati,
kimuktaṃ bhavati? vivakṣite maṇḍale sūryeṇāpūrite sati tadantaramapāntarālagamanena dvitīya maṇḍalaṃ saṃkrāmati saṃkamya ca tasmin maṇḍale cāraṃ carati|

Page #84
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
﻿________________

dvitīya prābhṛta - dvitīya prābhṛtaprābhṛta ]
2. tā maṃḍalāo maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe sūrie kaṇṇakalaṃ' nivveḍhei, evaiyaṃ ca ṇaṃ addhaṃ purao na gacchai, purao gacchamāṇe maṃḍalakālaṃ na parihavei, tesi ṇaṃ ayaṃ visese|
tattha ṇaṃ je te evamāhaṃsu -
maṃḍalāo maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe sūrie kaṇṇakalaṃ nivveḍhei eeṇaṃ ṭhāeṇaṃ ṇeyavvaṃ, ṇo ceva ṇaṃ iyareṇaṃ,
maṇḍalānmaṇḍalaṃ saṃkrāman saṃkramitumicchan sūryastadadhikṛtaṃ maṃḍalama prathamakṣaṇādṛrdhvamārabhya karṇa-kalaṃ nirveṣṭayati muṃcati, iyamatra bhāvanā-'bhārata airāvato vā sūryaḥ sva-svasthāne udgataḥ san aparamaṇḍalagataṃ karṇaṃ prathamakoṭibhāgarūpaṃ lakṣyīkṛtya śanaiḥśanairadhikṛtaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tayā kayācanāpi kalayā muṃcana cāraṃ carati' yena tasminnahorātre'tikrānte sati aparānantaramaṇḍalasyārambhe vartate iti| karṇakalamiti ca kriyāviśeṣaṇaṃ draṣṭavyaṃ, taccaivaṃ bhāvanīyaṃ - karṇa-aparamaṇḍalagataprathamakoṭibhāgarūpaṃ lakṣyīkṛtyādhikṛtamaṇḍalaṃ prathamakṣaṇādūrdhva kṣaṇe kṣaṇe kalayā'tikrāntaṃ yathā bhavati tathā nirveṣṭayatīti| - sūrya. ṭīkā.

23. tā kevaiyaṃ te khettaṃ sūrie egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai ? āhie tti vaejā| tattha khalu imāo cattāri paḍivattīo paṇṇattāo, taṃ jahā - tatthege evamāhaṃsu - (1) tā cha cha joyaṇasahassāī sūrie egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai, ege evamāhaṃsu - ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu - (2) tā paṃca paṃca joyaṇasahassāī sūrie egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai, ege evamāhaṃsu - ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu - (3) tā cattāri cattāri joyaṇasahassāiṃ sūrie egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai, ege evamāhaṃsu - ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu - (4) tā chavi, paṃca vi, catāri vi joyaṇasahassāiṃ sūrie egamegeṇaṃ muhuteṇaṃ gacchai, ega evamāhesu| tattha ṇaṃ je te evamāhaṃsu - (1) tā cha cha joyaṇasahassāī sūrie egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai, te evamāhaṃsu -
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabbhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapatte ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, jahaṇṇiyā duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai| taṃsi ca ṇaṃ divasaṃsi ega joyaṇasayasahassaṃ aṭṭha ya joyaṇasahassāī tāvakkhette paṇṇatte|
___tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabāhiraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapattā ukkosiyā aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai jahannae duvālasamuhatte divase bhavai|
taṃsi ca divasaṃsi bābatari joyaṇasahassāī tāvakkhete paṇṇate, tayā ṇaṃ cha cha joyaṇasahassāiṃ sūrie egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai|

[ sūryaprajñaptisūtra
aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, jahaṇiyā duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai|
taṃsi ca divasaṃsi ekkāṇaui joyaṇasahassāī tāvakkhette paṇṇatte| ___tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabāhiraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapattā ukkosiyā aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, jahaṇṇae duvālasamuhutte divase bhavai| taṃsi ca ṇaṃ divasaṃsi egaṭṭhijoyaṇasahassāī tāvakkhette paṇṇatte| tayā ṇaṃ cha vi, paṃca vi, cattāri vi joyaṇasahassāī sūrie egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai, ege evamāhaṃsu |
vayaṃ puṇa evaṃ vayāmo - tā sāiregāiṃ paṃca paṃca joyaṇasahassāī sūrie egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai| . pra. - tattha ko heū ? tti vaejā|
u. - tā ayaṇṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve savvadīva-samuddāṇaṃ savvabbhaṃtarāe savvakhuḍḍāge vaṭṭe jāva joyaṇasayasahassamāyāma-vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ, tini joyaṇasahassāī doni ya sattāvīse joyaṇasae tinni kose, aṭṭhāvīsaṃ ca dhaṇusayaṃ, terasa ya aṃgulāī, addhaṃgulaṃ ca kiṃcivisesāhie parikkheveṇaṃ paṇṇatte|
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabbhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ paṃca paṃca joyaṇasahassāī doṇṇi ya ekkāvaṇṇe joyaṇasayāī egūṇatīsaṃ ca saṭṭhibhāe joyaṇassa egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai|
tayā ṇaṃ ihagayassa maṇūsassa sīyālīsāe joyaṇasahassehiṃ dohi ya tevaḍhehiṃ joyaṇasaehiṃ ekkavīsāe ya saṭṭhibhāgehiṃ joyaṇassa sūrie cakkhupphāsaṃ havvamāgacchai|
tayā uttamakaṭṭhapatte ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, jahaṇiyā duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai|
se nikkhamamāṇe sūrie ṇavaṃ saṃvacḍaraṃ ayamāṇe paḍhamaṃsi ahorattaṃsi abhiṃtarāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai|
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie abdheitarāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ paṃca paṃca joyaṇasahassāī doṇṇi ya ekkāvaṇṇe joyaṇasae sīyālīsaṃ ca saṭṭhibhāe joyaṇassa egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai|
tayā ṇaṃ ihagayassa maṇūsassa sīyālīsāe joyaṇasahassehiṃ egūṇāsie ya joyaṇasae sattāvaṇṇāe saṭṭhibhāgehiṃ joyaṇassa saṭṭhibhāgaṃ ca egaṭṭhihā chettā egūṇavīsāe cuṇṇiābhāgehiṃ sūrie cakkhupphāsaṃ havvamāgacchai|
tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭhibhāga muhuttehiṃ ūṇe| duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai dohiṃ egaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahiyā| se nikkhamamāṇe sūrie doccaṃsi ahorattaṃsi abhiṃtaraṃ taccaṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai|

___tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie abhiṃtaraṃ taccaṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ paṃca paṃca joyaṇasahassāī doṇṇi ya bāvaṇṇe joyaṇasae paṃca ya saṭṭhibhāe joyaṇassa egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai|
tayā ṇaṃ ihagayassa maṇūsassa sīolīsāe joyaṇasahassehiṃ chaṇṇauīe ya joyaṇehiṃ tettīsāe ya saṭṭhibhāgehiṃ joyaṇassa saṭṭhibhāgaṃ ca egaṭṭhihā chettā dohiṃ cuṇṇiābhāgehiṃ sūrie cakkhupphāsaṃ havvamāgacchai|
tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, cauhiṃ egaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ūṇe| duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, cauhiṃ egaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahiyā|
evaṃ khalu eeṇaṃ uvāeṇaṃ nikkhamamāṇe sūrie tayāṇaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo tayāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe aṭṭhārasa aṭṭhārasa saṭṭhibhāge joyaṇassa egamege maṃḍale muhuttagaī abhivuḍḍhemāṇe abhivuḍḍhemāṇe culasīyaṃ culasīyaṃ sīyāiṃ joyaṇāī purisacchāyaṃ nivvuḍḍhemāṇe nivvuḍḍhemāṇe savvabāhiraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai|
. tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabāhiraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ paṃca paṃca joyaṇasahassāī tini ya paṃcuttare joyaṇasae paṇṇarasa ya saṭṭhibhāge joyaṇassa egamegeṇaṃ muhutteṇaṃ gacchai|
tayāṇaṃ ihagayassa maṇūsassa ekkatīsāe joyaṇasahassehiṃ aṭṭhahiṃ ekkatīsehiṃ joyaṇasahassehi tīsāe ya saṭṭhibhāgehiṃ joyaṇassa sūrie cakkhupphāsaṃ havvamāgacchai|

__ (ka ) tīse ṇaṃ savvabbhaṃtariyā bāhā = maṃdarapavvayaṃ teṇaṃ ṇava joyaṇasahassāī cattāri ya chalasīe joyaṇasae ṇava ya dasabhāge joyaṇassa parikkheveṇaṃ āhie tti vaejā|

__ (kha) tīse ṇaṃ savvabāhiriyā bāhā = lavaṇa samudaṃteṇaṃ, cauṇauiṃ joyaṇasahassāiṃ, aṭṭha ya aṭṭhasaṭhe joyaṇasae, cattāri ya dasabhāge joyaṇassa parikkheveṇaṃ, āhie tti vaejjā|

tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, cauhiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamahuttehiṃ ūṇe | 'duvālasamuhuttā rāī bhavai - cauhiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahiyā |
4. evaṃ khalu eeṇaṃ uvāeṇaṃ nikkhamamāṇe sūrie tayāṇaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo tayāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe egamege maṃḍale, egamegeṇaṃ rāīdieṇaṃ egamegaṃ egamegaṃ bhāgaṃ oyāe divasa - khettassa nivvuḍḍhemāṇe nivvuḍḍhemāṇe rayaṇi khettassa abhivuḍḍhemāṇe abhivuḍ ḍhemāṇe savvabāhiraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai |
5. tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie savvabdhaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo savvabāhiraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ savvabdhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ paṇihāya egeṇaṃ tesieṇaṃ rāiṃdiyasaeṇaṃ egaṃ tesīyabhāgasayaṃ oyāe divasa khettassa nivvuḍḍhettā rayaṇi khettassa abhivuḍḍhettā cāraṃ caraḍa़, maṃḍalaṃ aṭṭhārasehiṃ tīsehiṃ saehiṃ chettā |
tayā ṇaṃ uttamakaṭṭhapattā ukkosiyā aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, jahaṇṇae duvālasamuhutte divase
bhavai |
esa ṇaṃ paḍhame chammāse, esa ṇaṃ paḍhamassa chammāsassa, pajjavasāṇe |
1. se pavisamāṇe sūrie doccaṃ chammāsaṃ ayamāṇepaḍhamaṃsi ahorattaṃsi bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai |
tājā ṇaṃ sūrie bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ caraḍa़, tayā ṇaṃ egeṇaṃ rāīdieṇaṃ egaṃ bhāgaṃ oyāe rayaṇikhettassa nivvuḍḍhettā, divasa- khettassa abhivuḍḍhettā cāraṃ caraḍa़, maṃḍalaṃ aṭṭhārasehiṃ sehiṃ saehiṃ chettā |
tayā ṇaṃ aṭṭhārasamuhuttā rāī bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭibhāgamahuttehiṃ ūṇā |

[ sūryaprajñaptisūtra
duvālasamuhutte divase bhavai, dohiṃ egaṭṭhibhāgamuhuttehiṃ ahie| 2. se pavisamāṇe sūrie doccaṃsi ahorattaṃsi bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ taccaṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai|
tā jayā ṇaṃ sūrie bāhirāṇaṃtaraṃ taccaṃ maṃḍalaṃ tavasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai, tayā ṇaṃ dohiṃ rāiṃdiehiṃ do bhāe oyāe rayaṇikhettassa nivvuḍḍhettā, divasa-khettassa abhivuḍḍhettā cāraṃ carai, maṃḍalaṃ aṭṭhārasehiṃ tīsehiṃ saehiṃ chettā|

3. evaṃ khalu eeṇaṃ uvāeṇaṃ pavisamāṇe sūrie tayāṇaṃtarāo maṃḍalāo tayāṇaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ saṃkamamāṇe saṃkamamāṇe egamege maṃḍale, egamegeṇaṃ rāidieṇaṃ egamegaṃ bhāgaṃ oyāe rayaṇi-khettassa nivvuḍḍhemāṇe nivvaḍaḍhemāṇe divasa-khettassa abhivaḍaḍhemāṇe abhivaḍḍhemāṇe savvabbhaṃtaraṃ maṃḍalaṃ uvasaṃkamittā cāraṃ carai|

saptama prābhṛta sūriyeṇa pagāsiyā pavvayā
pa. - tā kiṃ te sūriyaṃ varai ? āhietti vaejjā| u. - tattha khalu imāo vīsaṃ paḍivattīo paṇṇattao taṃ jahā - tatthege evamāhaṃsu - 1. tā maṃdare ṇaṃ pavvae sūriyaṃ varai, ege evamāhaṃsu| ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu - 2. tā merū ṇaṃ pavvae sūriyaṃ varai ege evamāhaṃsu| 3-19. evaṃ eeṇaṃ abhilāveṇaṃ ṇeyavvaṃ taheva jāva| ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu - 20. tā pavvayarāye ṇaṃ pavvae sūriyaṃ varai, ege evamāhaṃsu| vayaṃ puṇa evaṃ badāmo -
tā maṃdare ṇaṃ pavvae sūriyaṃ varai, evaṃ pi pavuccai taheva jāva (1-20 sūriya. pā. 5, su. 26 ko dekheṃ)|
tā pavvayarāye ṇaṃ pavvae sūriyaṃ varai, evaṃ pi pavuccai| (ka) tā je ṇaṃ poggalā sūriyassa lesaṃ phusaṃti, te ṇaṃ puggalā sūriyaṃ varayaṃti| (kha) adiṭṭhā vi ṇaṃ poggalā sūriyaṃ varayaṃti| (ga) carimalessaṃtaragayā viṇaṃ poggalā sūriyaṃ varayaṃti|
1. 'sūriyassa lessā paḍidhāyagā pavvayā' isa śīrṣaka ke antargata sūrya. prā. 5, su. 26 meṃ bīsa pratipattiyoṃ ke anusāra sūrya kī leśyā | ko pratihata karane vāle bīsa parvatoṃ ke nāma gināye haiṃ| yahāṃ bhī usī ke anusāra mūla-pāṭha ke sabhī ālāpaka kahane cāhiye| 2. ūpara ke ṭippaṇa meṃ sūcita śīrṣaka ke antargata sūrya. pā.5,su. 26 ke anusāra sūryaprajñapti ke saṃkalanakartā ne yahāṃ bhī maṃdara parvata ke bīsa nāmoṃ ko paryāyavācī mānakara samanvaya kara liyā hai|

(ka) tā jayā ṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve dāhiṇaḍḍhe aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, tayā ṇaṃ uttaraḍḍhe'vi aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai|
jayā ṇaṃ uttaraḍḍhe aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, tayā ṇaṃ dāhiṇaḍḍhe'vi aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai|

tā jaṃbuddīve dīve sūriyā| udīṇa-pāīṇamuggacchaṃti, pāīṇa-dāhiṇamāgacchaṃti| pāīṇa-dāhiṇamuggacchaṃti, dāhiṇa-paḍīṇamāgacchaṃti| dāhiṇa-paḍīṇamuggacchaṃti, paḍīṇa-udīṇamāgacchaṃti| paḍīṇa-udīṇamuggacchaṃti, udīṇa-pāīṇamāgacchaṃti|'
(ka) tā jayā ṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve maṃdarassa pavvayassa dāhiṇaḍḍhe divase bhavai, tayā ṇaṃ uttaraḍḍhe'vi divase bhavai|
jayā ṇaṃ uttaraḍḍhe divase bhavai, tayā ṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve maṃdarassa pavvayassa purathima-paccitthame ṇaṃ rāī bhavai|

___ jayā ṇaṃ paccatthime ṇaṃ divase bhavai, tayā ṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve maṃdarassa pavvayassa uttaradāhiṇe ṇaṃ rāī bhavai|
(ka) tā jayā ṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve maṃdarassa pavvayassa dāhiṇaḍḍhe ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavaī, tayā ṇaṃ uttaraḍḍhe ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai|
__ jayā ṇaṃ uttaraḍḍhe ukkosae aṭṭhārasamuhutte divase bhavai, tayā ṇaṃ jaṃbuddīve dīve maṃdarassa pavvayassa
1. pa. jambūddīve ṇaṃ bhaṃte ! dīve sūriā ? udīṇapāīṇamuggaccha pāīṇadāhiṇamāgacchaṃti ?
pāīṇadāhiṇamuggaccha dāhiṇapaḍīṇamāgacchaṃ ti ? dāhiṇapaḍīṇamuggaccha paḍīṇaudīṇamāgacchaṃti ?

navama prābhṛta porisicchāya-nivvattaṇaṃ 30. pa. tā kaikaṭṭha te sūrie porisicchāyaṃ ṇivvetti ? āhie tti vaejjā,
u. tattha khalu imāo tiṇṇi paḍivattīo paṇṇattāo, taṃ jahā - tatthege evamāhaṃsu -
1. tāje ṇaṃ poggalā sūriyassa lesaṃ phusaṃti, te ṇaṃ poggalā saṃtappaṃti, te ṇaṃ poggalā saṃtappamāṇā tadaṇaṃtarāiṃ bāhirāī poggalāī saṃtāveṃtīti,
esa ṇaṃ se samie tāvakkhette ege evamāhaṃsu, eo puṇa evamāhaṃsu -
2. tā je ṇaṃ poggalā sūriyassa lesaṃ phusaṃti, te ṇaṃ poggalā no saṃtappaṃti, te ṇaṃ poggalā . asaṃtappamāṇā tadaṇaṃtarāī bāhirāī poggalāī saṃtāveṃtīti, -
esa ṇaṃ se samie tāvakkhette ege evamāhaṃsu, ege puṇa evamāhaṃsu -
3. tā je ṇaṃ poggalā sūriyassa lesaṃ phusaṃti, te ṇaṃ poggalā atthegaiyā saṃtappaṃti, atthegaiyā no saṃtappaṃti,
tattha atthegaiyā saṃtappamāṇā tadaṇaṃtarāī bāhirāī poggalāī atthegayāiṃ saṃtāveṃti, atthegayāiṃ no saṃtāvetīti,
esa ṇaṃ se samie tāvakkhette ege evamāhaṃsu, vayaṃ puṇa evaṃ vayāmo -
tājāo imāo caṃdima-sūriyāṇaṃ devāṇaṃ vimāṇehiṃto lesāo bahittā ucchūḍhā abhiṇisaṭṭhāo patāveṃti,
eyāsi ṇaṃ lesāṇaṃ aṃtaresu aṇṇayarīo chiṇṇalesāo saṃmucchaṃti, tae ṇaṃ tāo chiṇṇalessāo saṃmucchiyāo samāṇīo tadaṇaṃtarāiṃ bāhirāī poggalāī saṃtāveṃtīti,
esa ṇaṃ se samie tāvakkhette|

daśamaprābhṛta [ gyārahavā prābhṛtaprābhṛta ]
tā kahaṃ te caṃdamaggā āhie tti vaejjā ? tāsi ṇaṃ aṭṭhāvīsāe ṇakkhattāṇaṃ
-
1. atthi ṇakkhattā jeṇaṃ sayā caṃdassa dāhiṇeṇaṃ jogaṃ joeṃti, 2. atthi ṇakkhattā je ṇaṃ sayā caṃdassa uttareṇaṃ jogaṃ joeṃti, 3. atthi ṇakkhattā je ṇaṃ caṃdassa dāhiṇeṇa'vi uttareṇa'vi jogaṃ joeṃti,
4. atthi ṇakkhattā jeṇaṃ caṃdassa dāhiṇeṇa'vi pamaddapi jogaṃ joeṃti, 5. atthi ṇakkhattā je ṇaṃ caṃdassa sayā pamaddaṃ jogaṃ joeṃti,
tāeesi ṇaṃ aṭṭhāvīsāe ṇakkhattāṇaṃ
1. kayare ṇakkhattā je ṇaṃ sayā caṃdassa dāhiṇeṇaṃ jogaṃ joeṃti ?
2. kayare ṇakkhattā jeṇaṃ sayā caṃdassa uttareṇaṃ jogaṃ joeṃti ? 3. kayare ṇakkhattā je ṇaṃ caṃdassa dāhiṇeṇa'vi uttareṇa'vi jogaṃ joeṃti ?
4. kayare ṇakkhattā je ṇaṃ sayā caṃdassa dāhiṇeṇa'vi pamaddaṃ jogaṃ joeṃti ? 5. kayare ṇakkhattā je ṇaṃ caṃdassa sayā pamaddaṃ jogaṃ joeṃti ?
u. 1. tāeesi ṇaṃ aṭṭhāvīsāe ṇakkhattāṇaṃ

vikkhaṃbho, te ṇaṃ bārasa, taṃjahā - 1. do satabhisayā, 2. do bharaṇī, 3. do addā, 4. do assesā, 5. do sātī, 6. do jeṭṭhā| ga. tattha je te ṇakkhattā, jesi ṇaṃ do sahassā dasuttarā sattasaṭṭhibhāga tīsai bhāge ṇaṃ sīmāvikkhaṃbho, te ṇaṃ tīsaṃ, taṃjahā - 1. do savaṇā, 2. do dhaṇiṭṭhā, 3. do puvvābhaddavayā, 4. do revaī, 5. do assiṇī, 6. do kattiyā,7. do saṃṭhāṇā, 8. do pussā, 9. do mahā, 10. do puvvāphagguṇī, 11. do hatthā, 12. do cittā, 13. do aṇurādhā, 14. do mūlā, 15. do puvvāsāḍhā, gha. tattha je te ṇakkhattā, jesi ṇaṃ tiṇṇi sahassā paṇṇarasuttarā sattasaṭṭhibhāga tīsai bhāge

tṛtīyasyāḥ paurṇamāsyāḥ parato dvādaśī kila paurṇamāsī navamī bhavati,
tato navabhitriṃśato guṇane dve śate aṣṭāśītyadhike bhavataḥ| 2. 1. 'jaṃbuddīvassa ṇamityādi'
jaṃbuddīpasya ṇamiti vākyālaṃkāre dvīpasyopari prācīnā prācīnatayā, iha prācīnagrahaṇenottarapūrvā (īśāna) gṛhyate, apācīnagrahaṇena dakṣiṇāparā, (naiṛtya)| tato'yamarthaḥ pūrvottara-dakṣiṇāparāyatayā, evamudīci-dakṣiṇāyatayā, pūrva-dakṣiṇottarāparāyatayā jīvayā pratyaṃcayā davarikayā ityarthaḥ, maṇḍalaṃ caturviṃśena-caturviṃśatyadhikena śatena chittvā vibhajya bhūyaścaturbhivibhajyate, tato dākṣiṇātye caturbhāgamaṇḍale ekatriṃśadbhāgapramāṇe saptaviṃśatibhāgānupādāyāṣṭāviṃśatitamaṃ ca bhāgaṃ viṃśatithā chittvā tadgatānaṣṭādaśabhāgānupādāya śestribhirbhāgaiścaturthasya bhāgasya dvābhyāṃ kalābhyāṃ, pāścātyaṃ caturbhāgamaṇḍalamasaṃprāptaḥ asmin pradeśe candro dvāṣaṣṭitamāṃ caramā paurṇimāsī parisamāpayati| - ṭīkā

u. tā uttarāhiṃ āsāḍhāhiṃ, uttarāṇaṃ āsāḍhāṇaṃ cattālīsaṃ muhuttā, cattālīsaṃ ya bāsaṭṭhibhāgā

103. pa. tā kahaṃ te rāhukamme ? āhie tti vaejā ?

tattha je te evamāhaṃsu -

tattha ṇaṃ ime paṇṇarasakasiṇapoggalā, paṇṇattā, taṃjahā - 1. siṃghāṇae, 2. jaḍilae, 3. kharae, 4. khatae, 5. aṃjaṇe, 6. khaṃjaṇe, 7. sītale, 8. himasītale, 9. kelāse, 10. aruṇābhe, 11. parijjae, 12. ṇabhasūrae, 13. kavilie, 14. piṃgalae, 15. rāhū |
tā jayā ṇaṃ ee paṇṇarasa kasiṇā kasiṇā poggalā sayā caṃdassa vā sūrassa vā lesāṇubaddhacāriṇo bhavati, tayā ṇaṃ māṇusaloyaṃsi māṇusā evaṃ vayaṃti - 'evaṃ khalu rāhū caṃdaṃ vā sūraṃ vā geṇhai, evaṃ tā jayā ṇaṃ ee paṇṇarasa kasiṇā kasiṇā poggalā ṇo sayā caṃdassa vā surassa vā lesāvubaddhacāriṇo bhavaṃti ṇo

ogāhai kevaiyaṃ 5, kevaiyaṃ ca vikaṃpai 6| maṃḍalāṇa ya saṃṭhāṇe 7, vikkhaṃbho 8, aṭṭha pāhuḍā||11|| sūtra 2 chappaṃca ya satteva ya, aṭṭha ya tini ya havaṃti paḍivattī| paḍhamassa pāhuḍassa u, havaṃti eyāo paḍivattī||12|| sūtra 3 paḍivattīo udae, aduva atthamaṇesu ya| bheyaghāe kaṇṇakalā, muhuttāṇa gaīi ya||13|| nikkhamamāṇe sigghagaī, pavisaṃte maṃdagaīi ya| culasīisayaṃ purisāṇaṃ, tesiṃ ca paḍivattīo||14|| udayammi aṭṭha bhaṇiyā, bheyagyāe duve ca paḍivattī| cattāri muhuttagaīe, hoti taiyammi paḍivattī|| 15|| sūtra 4 āvaliya 1, muhuttagge 2, evaṃbhāgā 3 ya, jogassā 4| kulāiṃ 5, puṇṇamāsī 6 ya, saṃnivāe 7 ya saṃṭhiī 8||16|| tāraggaṃ 9, caṇetā ya, 10, caṃdamaggatti 11 yāvare| devatāṇaṃ ya ajjhayaṇā 12, muhuttāṇaṃ nāmayā 13 iya||17|| divasā rāī ya vuttā 14 ya, tihi 15, gottā 16bhoyaṇāṇi ya 17| āiccacāra 18, māsā 19 ya, paṃca saṃvaccharā 20 iya||18|| joisassa ya dārāī 21,
nakkhatavicae 22 viya| dasame pāhuḍe ee,
bāvīsaṃ pāhuḍa-pāhuḍā||19|| sūtra 5 teṇaṃ kāleṇaṃ teṇaṃ samaeṇaṃ mihilā ṇāmaṃ ṇayarī hotthā riddhasthimiyasamiddhā pamuiyajaṇajāṇavayā jāva pāsādīyā, vaṇṇao||1||
tīse ṇaṃ mihilāe ṇayarīe bahiyā uttarapurasthime disībhāe ettha ṇaṃ māṇibhadde ṇāmaṃ ceie hotthā cirāīe, vaṇṇao||2||
tīse ṇaṃ mihilāe ṇayarīe jiyasattūṇāmaṃ rāyā, dhāriṇī devī, vaṇṇao||3||
teṇaṃ kāleṇaṃ teṇaṃ samaeṇaṃ taṃmi māṇibhadde ceie sāmī samosaḍhe, parisā ṇiggayā, dhammo kahio, parisā paḍigayā jāva rāyā jāmeva disiṃ pāubbhūe tāmeva disiṃ paḍigae||4|| sūtra 6||
teṇaṃ kāleṇaṃ teṇaṃ samaeṇaṃ samaṇassa bhagavao mahāvīrassa jeṭhe aṃtevāsī iṃdabhūī ṇāmaṃ aṇagāre goyamagotte ṇaṃ sattussehe jāva pajuvāsamāṇe evaṃ vayāsī || sūtra 7||

dūsare maṃḍala kī paridhi meṃ 18 yojana milāne para tīsare maṃḍala kī paridhi ātī hai|
isa prakāra pratyeka maṃḍala kī paridhi jñāta kī jā sakatī hai|
pratyeka maṃḍala meṃ sūrya kī eka muhūrta meṃ kitanī gativṛddhi hotī hai, yaha jānane ke liye isa sūtra kā upayoga karanā cāhiye -
pratyeka maṃḍala meṃ paridhi kī vṛddhi / 60 muhūrta|
pratyeka maṃḍala meṃ 18 yojana paridhi meṃ vṛddhi hotī hai| use 60 muhūrta se bhāga dene para 1 muhūrta meṃ hone vālī gativṛddhi prāpta hogī|
18 yojana pratyeka maṃḍala kī paridhi meṃ hone vālī vṛddhi /60 muhūrta =18/60 yojana muhūrta meṃ gati meṃ vṛddhi hotī hai| sūrya ke dṛṣṭipatha kṣetra kā aṃtara jñāta karane kī vidhi
usa-usa maṃḍala meṃ vidyamāna sūrya dṛṣṭipatha ke kṣetra kā aṃtara jñāta karane ke liye nimnalikhita sūtra kā upayoga karanā cāhiye -
sūrya kī usa-usa maṃḍala meṃ eka muhūrta kī gati 4 dinamāna kā ardhabhāga|
sarvābhyaṃtara maṃḍala meṃ sūrya ke dṛṣṭipatha kṣetra kā pramāṇa 47263 yojana 21/60 bhāga hai | usako jānane ke liye uparyukta sūtra kā upayoga karane para - 5251 yojana 29/60 bhāga|

|| dūsarā prābhṛta samāpta || sūtra 24 sūrya dvārā prakāśamāna kṣetra kā pramāṇa
___jaba sūrya sarvābhyaṃtara maṃḍala meṃ vartamāna hotā hai taba jaṃbūdvīpa ke kalpita pāṃca cakravāla meṃ se ḍeḍha bhāga prakāśita karatā hai aura eka bhāga aprakāśita hotā hai|
jambūdvīpa meṃ vartamāna donoṃ sūrya kī apekṣā pāṃca cakravāla meṃ se tīna bhāga prakāśita karate haiṃ aura do bhāga aprakāśita hote haiṃ | arthāt jambūdvīpa ke kalpita pāṃca bhāga meṃ se tīna bhāga dina hotā hai aura do bhāga rātri hotī hai|
___ jambūdvīpa ke 366 bhāga kī kalpanā karane para 1 bhāga (cakravāla) ke 732 bhāga hote haiṃ , 3 cakravāla ke 2196 bhāga hote haiṃ | arthāt 3660 bhāga meṃ se 2196 bhāga kā divasa hotā hai 1464 bhāga rātri hotī hai, athavā donoṃ sūrya 60 muhūrta meṃ 1 maṃḍala kī parikramā pūrṇa karate haiṃ | jambūdvīpa ke 5 cakravāla kī kalpanā karane para 1 cakravāla 12 muhūrtātmaka hotā hai | 12 muhūrta kā kāla jambūdvīpa ke 3660 bhāga kī kalpanā meṃ 732 bhāgātmaka hotā hai|
sarvābhyaṃtara maṃḍala se sūrya jaba sarvabāhyamaṃḍala kī ora gamana karatā hai taba pratimaṃḍala meṃ ahorātra meṃ 2| 61 bhāga hāni-vṛddhi hotī hai|
. sarvābhyaṃtaramaṃḍala ko kama karane para sarvabāhyamaṃḍala 183 vāṃ ātā hai| jisase 18342 karane para 366/61 bhāga kī hāni-vṛddhi ahorātra meṃ hotī hai| arthāt 6 muhūrta divasa meṃ hāni aura rātri meṃ vṛddhi hotī hai | donoṃ sūrya kī apekṣā 12 muhūrta kī hāni vṛddhi hotī hai| sarvabāhyamaṃḍala meṃ sūrya eka bhāga prakāśita karatā hai aura ḍeḍha़ bhāga aprakāśita rahatā hai|
pūrva meṃ batāye gaye anusāra eka-eka sūrya kī apekṣā 1 bhāga divasa aura ḍeḍha़ bhāga rātri rahatī hai| donoṃ sūrya kī apekṣā 2 bhāga divasa aura 3 bhāga rātri hotī hai|

muhūrta
uparyukta koṣṭhaka nakṣatra kā caṃdra ke sātha kitane muhūrta kā yoga hotā hai, yaha batalāne ke liye hai| nakṣatroṃ kā sūrya ke sātha yoga nakṣatra saṃkhyā sūrya ke sātha yoga
nakṣatroṃ kā nāma divasa
abhijita śatabhiṣā, bharaṇī, ādrā, āśleṣā, svāti, jyeṣṭhā śravaṇa, dhaniṣṭhā, pūrvā, bhā. revatī, aśvinī kṛtikā, mṛgaśira, puṣya, maghā; pū. phā.
hasta, citrā, anurādhā, mūla, pūrvāṣāḍha़ā 203
u. bhā., rohiṇī, punarvasu, u. phā.,
viśākhā, u. ṣāḍha़ā| jo nakṣatra candramā ke sātha 1 divasa ke 67 bhāga meṃ se athavā usase viśeṣa jitane bhāga gamana karatā hai, usake pā~caveṃ bhāga pramāṇa sūrya ke sātha divasa aura muhūrta ke parimāṇa se gamana karatā hai|
jaise ki abhijita nakṣatra candra ke sātha 21 | 67 bhāga gamana karatā hai to sūrya ke sātha kitanā gaṃmana karatā hai ? yaha jānane ke liye 21 bhāga ko 5 se bhāga dene para 4 divasa 1 | 5 bhāga muhūrta āte haiṃ| 1|5 muhūrta nikālane ke liye 30 se guṇā karane para 6 muhūrta āte haiṃ| jisakā āśaya yaha huā ki abhijita nakṣatra sūrya ke sātha 4 divasa 6 muhūrta yoga karatā hai| isa prakāra anya sthāna para bhī samajhanā cāhiye| 15 muhūrta candra ke sātha yoga karane para nakṣatra kā sūrya ke sātha yogakāla
śatabhiṣā nakṣatra candra ke sātha 1 divasa ke 67 bhāga meṃ se 33% bhāga yoga karatā hai, to sūrya ke sātha kitane divasa aura kitane muhūrta yoga karatā hai ?
ataḥ 6 divasa 7 | 10 muhūrta sūrya ke sātha yoga karatā hai|
1. ke muhūrta jānane ke liye 30 se guṇā karane para 21 muhūrta āte haiṃ | arthāt śatabhiṣā nakṣatra kā sūrya ke sātha 6 divasa aura 21 muhūrta yoga hotā hai|
isī prakāra anya nakṣatroṃ ke liye jānanā cāhiye|

sūryaprajñaptisūtra
sūtra 20 va 24 sūramaṃḍalassa āyāma-vikkhaṃbho parikkhevo bāhallaṃ ca pa. sūramaṃḍale ṇaṃ bhaṃte! kevaiyaṃ āyāma-vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ kevaiyaṃ parikkheveṇaṃ kevaiyaṃ bāhalleṇaṃ paṇṇatte ? u. goyamā! sūramaṃḍale aḍayālīsaṃ egasaṭṭhibhāe joyaṇassa āyāma-vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ taṃ tiguṇaṃ savisesaṃ parikkheveṇaṃ cauvīsaṃ egasaṭṭhibhāe joyaṇassa bāhalleṇaṃ paṇṇatte|
- jaṃbu. vakkha.7, su. 130 jaṃbuddīve sūriyā paḍuppannaṃ khettaṃ obhāsaṃti pa. jaṃbuddīve ṇaṃ bhaṃte! dīve sūriyā kiṃ tīyaṃ khettaṃ obhāsaṃti, paḍupannaṃ khettaṃ obhāsaṃti, aṇāgayaṃ khettaṃ
obhāsaṃti?
goyamā! no tīyaṃ khettaṃ obhāseṃti, paḍupanaṃ khettaṃ obhāseṃti, no aṇāgayaṃ khettaṃ obhāseṃti| pa. taṃ bhaṃte! kiṃ puḍheṃ obhāseṃti, apuḍheṃ obhāseṃti ? u. goyamā! puḍheṃ obhāseṃti, no puḍhaṃ obhāseṃti jāva| pa. taṃ bhaṃte! kiṃ egadisiṃ obhāseṃti, chaddirsi obhāseṃti ? 1. (ka) sūramaṃḍale ṇaṃ aḍayālīsaṃ egasaṭṭhibhāe joyaṇassa vikkhaṃbheṇaṃ paṇṇatte, - sama. 48, su. 3
(kha) sūramaṃḍalaṃ joyaṇe ṇaṃ terasahiṃ egaṭṭhibhāehiṃ joyaṇassa ūṇaṃ paṇṇattaṃ, - sama. 13, su.8 2. yāvat pada se saṃgrahīta sūtra pa. taṃ bhaṃte ! kiṃ ogāḍhaṃ obhāseṃti, aṇogāḍhaṃ obhāseṃti ?
goyamā! ogāḍhaṃ obhāseṃti, no aṇogāḍhaṃ obhāseṃti, pa. taṃ bhaṃte ! kiṃ aṇaṃtarogāḍhaṃ obhāseṃti, paraṃparogāḍhaṃ obhāseṃti ? u. goyamā! aṇaṃtarogāḍhaṃ obhāseṃti, no paraṃparogāḍhaṃ obhāseṃti, pa. taṃ bhaṃte ! kiṃ aṇuṃ obhāseṃti, bāyaraṃ obhāseṃti ?
goyamā! aṇuṃ pi obhāseṃti, bāyaraṃ pi obhāseṃti,
taṃ bhaṃte ! kiṃ uḍḍhe obhāseṃti, tiriyaṃ obhāseṃti ahe obhāseṃti? u. goyamā! uḍḍhe pi, tiriyaṃ pi, ahe vi obhāseṃti| pa. . taṃ bhaṃte ! kiṃ āī obhāseṃti, majjhe obhāseṃti aṃte obhāseṃti ? u. goyamā! āī pi, majjhevi, aṃte vi obhāseṃti,
taṃ bhaṃte ! kiṃ savisae obhāseṃti, avisae obhāseṃti ? ___ goyamā! savisae obhāseṃti, no avisae obhāseṃti,
(kramaśaḥ)

u. goyamā! no egadisiṃ obhāseṃti, niyamā chaddisiṃ obhāseṃti| viyā. sa.8, u.8, su. 39, 40 jaṃbuddīve sūriyā paḍuppannaṃ khettaṃ ujjoveti | pa. jaṃbuddīve ṇaṃ bhaṃte! dīve sūriyā kiṃ tīyaṃ khettaṃ ujoveṃti paḍuppannaṃ khettaṃ ujjoveṃti, aṇāgayaṃ khettaṃ
ujjoveṃti ? u. goyamā! no tīye khettaṃ ujoveṃti, paḍuppannaṃ khettaṃ ujjoveṃti, no aṇāgayaṃ khettaṃ ujoveṃti, evaṃ taveṃti, evaṃ bhāsaṃti jāva niyamā chaddisiṃ bhāsaṃti| ___jaṃbuddīve sūriyāṇaṃ tāva khettaṃ pamāṇaṃ
- viyā. sa.8, u.8,su. 41-42 pa. jaṃbuddīveṇaṃ bhaṃte ! dīve sūriyā kevaiyaṃ khettaṃ uḍḍhaṃ tavaṃti ? kevaiyaṃ khettaṃ ahe tavaṃti ? kevaiyaṃ khettaṃ
tiriyaṃ tavaṃti? u. goyamā! egaṃ joyaṇasayaṃ uḍḍhaṃ tavaṃti, aṭṭhārasajoyaṇasayāī ahe tavaṃti, sīyālīsaṃ joyaṇasahassāī doṇṇi tevaḍhe joyaṇasae ekkavīsaṃ ca saṭṭhibhāe joyaṇassa tiriyaṃ tavaṃti|
- viyā sa. 8, u.8, su. 45
pa. taṃ bhaṃte ! āṇupuvviṃ obhāseṃti, no aṇāṇupuvviṃ obhāseṃti ? u. goyamā! āṇupuṭviṃ obhāseṃti, no aṇāṇupuvviṃ obhāseṃti, pa. taṃ bhaṃte ! kai disiṃ obhāseṃti ? u. goyamā! niyamā chaddisiṃ obhāseṃti, -viyā. sa.8, u.8, su. 39 ṭippaṇa [pa.taṃ bhaṃte ! kiṃ egadisiṃ obhāseṃti, saddirsi obhāseṃti ? u. goyamā! no egadisiṃ obhāseṃti, niyamā chaddisiṃ obhāseti|] (pāṭhāntara) 1.jaṃbu. vakkha.7, su, 137 2.jaṃbu. vakkha.7, su. 137 3. (ka) jaṃbu. vakkha. 7, 139
(kha) sūriya. pā. 4, su. 25 sūrya ke vimāna se sau yojana ūpara śanaiścara graha kā vimāna hai aura vahīṃ taka jyotiṣa cakra kī sīmā hai, ata: isase
ūpara sūrya kā tāpakṣetra nahīṃ hai| 4. jaṃbudvīpa ke paścima mahāvideha se jayaṃtadvāra kī ora lavaṇasamudra ke samīpa kramaśa: eka hajāra yojana paryanta bhūmi nīce hai, isa apekṣā se eka hajāra yojana tathā meru ke samīpa kī samabhūmi se 800 yojana ū~cā sūrya kā vimāna hai, ye āṭha sau yojana saṃyukta karane para aṭhāraha sau yojana sūrya vimāna se nīce kī ora kā tāpakṣetra hai, anya dvīpoṃ meṃ bhūmi sama rahatī hai| isaliye vahāṃ sūrya kā nīce kā tāpakṣetra kevala āṭha sau yojana kā hai| aṭhāraha sau yojana nīce kī ora ke tāpakṣetra ke aura sau yojana ūpara kī ora ke tāpakṣetra ke, ina donoṃ saṃkhyāoṃ ke saṃyukta karane para 1900 yojana kā sūrya kā tāpakṣetra hai| 5. yahāṃ tirache tāpakṣetra kā kathana pūrva-paścima diśā kī apekṣā se kahā gayā hai, arthāt utkṛṣṭa itanī dūrī para sthita sūrya mānava cakṣu se dekhā jā sakatā hai|
uttara meṃ 180 yojana nyūna paiṃtālāsa hajāra yojana tathā dakṣiṇadiśā meṃ dvīpa meṃ 180 yojana aura lavaṇasamudra meṃ teṃtīsa hajāra tīna sau tetīsa yojana tathā eka yojana ke tṛtīya bhāga saṃyukta dūrī se sūrya dekhā jā sakatā hai|

anadhyāyakāla (sva. ācāryapravara śrī ātmārāmajī ma. dvārā sampādita nandīsūtra se uddhṛta)
svadhyāya ke liye āgamoṃ meṃ jo samaya batāyā gayā hai, usī samaya śāstroṃ kā svādhyāya karanā cāhie| anadhyāyakāla meṃ svādhyāya varjita hai|
manusmṛti ādi smṛtiyoṃ meṃ bhī anadhyāyakāla kā vistārapūrvaka varṇana kiyā gayā hai| vaidika loga bhī veda ke anadhyāyoṃ kā ullekha karate haiṃ| isī prakāra anya ārṣa granthoṃ kā bhī anadhyāya mānā jātā hai| jaināgama bhī sarvajñokta, devādhiṣṭhita tathā svaravidyā saṃyukta hone ke kāraṇa, inakā bhī āgamoṃ meṃ anadhyāyakāla varṇita kiyā gayā hai, jaise ki -
dasavidhe aṃtalikkhite asajjhāe paṇṇatte, taṃ jahā - ukkāvāte, disidāghe, gajite, vijute, nigghāte, juvate, jakkhālitte, dhūmitā, mahitā, rayaugghāte|
dasavihe orālite asajjhātite, taṃ jahā - aṭṭhī, maṃsaṃ, soṇite, asutisāmaṃte, susāṇasāmaṃte, caṃdovarāte, sūrovarāte, paḍane, rāyavuggahe, uvassayassa aṃto orālie sarīrage|
- sthānāṅga sūtra, sthāna 10 no kappati niggaṃthāṇa vā, niggaṃthīṇa vā cauhiṃ māhāpāḍivaehiṃ sajjhāyaṃ karittae, taṃ jahā - āsāḍhapāḍivae, iṃdamahāpāḍivae, kattaapāḍivae sugimhapāḍivae| no kappai niggaṃthāṇa vā niggaṃthīṇa vā, cauhiṃ saṃjhāhiṃ sajjhāyaṃ karettae, taṃ jahā – paḍimāte, pacchimāte majjhaṇhe, aḍḍharatte| kappai niggaṃthāṇaṃ vā niggaṃthīṇa vā, cāukkālaṃ sajjhāyaṃ karettae, taṃ jahā - puvvaṇhe avaraṇhe, paose, paccūse|
- - sthānāṅga sūtra, sthāna 4, uddeśaka 2 uparyukta sūtrapāṭha ke anusāra, dasa ākāśa se sambandhita, dasa audārika śarīra se sambandhita, cāra mahāpratipadā, cāra mahāpratipadā kī pūrṇimā aura cāra saṃdhyā, isa prakāra battīsa anadhyāya māne gae haiṃ, jinakā saṃkṣepa meṃ nimna prakāra se varṇana hai, jaise - ākāśa sambandhī dasa anadhyāya
1. ulkāpāta-tārāpatana - yadi mahat tārāpatana huā hai to eka prahara paryanta śāstrasvādhyāya nahīṃ karanā cāhie|

anadhyāyakāla ]
[ 241
2. digdāha – jaba taka diśā raktavarṇa kī ho arthāt aisā mālūma paḍa़e ki diśā meṃ āga sī lagī hai taba bhī svādhyāya nahīṃ karanā cāhie|
3. garjita - bādaloṃ ke garjana para eka prahara paryanta svādhyāya na kare| 4. vidyuta - bijalī camakane para eka prahara paryanta svādhyāya na kare|
kintu garjana aura vidyuta kā asvādhyāya cāturmāsa meṃ nahīṃ mānanā cāhie| kyoṃki vaha garjana aura vidyuta prāyaḥ ṛtu-svabhāva se hī hotā hai| ataḥ ārdrā se svāti nakṣatra paryanta anadhyāya nahīṃ mānā jātā|
5. nirghāta - binā bādala ke ākāśa meṃ vyantarādikṛta ghora garjanā hone para, yā bādaloṃ sahita ākāśa meṃ kaḍa़kane para do prahara taka asvādhyāya kāla hai|
6. yūpaka - śuklapakṣa meṃ pratipadā, dvitīyā, tṛtīyā ko saṃdhyā kī prabhā aura candraprabhā ke milane ko yūpaka kahā jātā hai| ina dinoṃ prahara rātri paryanta svādhyāya nahīṃ karanā cāhie|
. 7. yakṣādīpta - kabhī kisī diśā meṃ bijalī camakane jaisā, thoḍa़e-thoḍa़e samaya pīche jo prakāśa hotā hai vaha yakṣādīpta kahalātā hai| ataḥ ākāśa meṃ jaba taka yakṣākāra dīkhatā rahe taba taka svādhyāya nahīṃ karanā cāhie|
8. dhūmikā-kṛṣṇa - kārtika se lekara māgha taka kā samaya meghoṃ kā gabhamāsa hotā hai| isameṃ dhūmra varṇa kī sūkṣma jalarūpa dhuṃdha paḍa़tī hai| vaha dhūmikā-kṛṣṇa kahalātī hai| jaba taka yaha dhuṃdha paḍa़tī rahe, taba taka svādhyāya nahīṃ karanā cāhie|
9. mihikāśveta – śītakāla meṃ śveta varṇa kī sūkṣma jalarūpa dhuṃdha mihikā kahalātī hai| jaba taka giratī rahe, taba taka asvādhyāyakāla hai|
10. raja-udghāta – vāyu ke kāraṇa ākāśa meṃ cāroṃ ora dhūli chā jātī hai| jaba taka yaha dhūli phailī rahatī hai, svādhyāya nahīṃ karanā cāhie|
uparokta dasa kāraṇa ākāśa sambandhī asvādhyāya ke haiṃ| audārika śarīra sambandhī dasa anadhyāya
11-12-13. haḍḍī, māṃsa aura rudhira - paṃcendriya tiryaṃca kī haḍḍī, māṃsa aura rudhira yadi sāmane dikhāī deṃ, to jaba taka vahā~ se yaha vastue~ uṭhāī na jāe~ taba taka asvādhyāya hai| vṛttikāra āsa-pāsa ke 60 hātha taka ina vastuoṃ ke hone para asvādhyāya mānate haiṃ|
isī prakāra manuṣya sambandhī asthi, māṃsa aura rudhira kā bhī anadhyāya mānā jātā hai| viśeṣatā itanī hai ki inakā asvādhyāya sau hātha taka tathā eka dina-rāta kā hotā hai| strī ke māsika dharma kā asvādhyāya tīna dina taka| bālaka evaṃ bālikā ke janma kā asvādhyāya kramaśaḥ sāta evaṃ āṭha dina

14. aśuci - mala-mūtra sāmane dikhāī dene taka asvādhyāya hai| 15. śmaśāna – śmaśānabhūmi ke cāroṃ ora sau-sau hātha paryanta asvādhyāya mānā jātā hai|
16. candragrahaṇa - candragrahaṇa hone para jaghanya āṭha, madhyama bāraha aura utkṛṣṭa solaha prahara paryanta svādhyāya nahīṃ karanā cāhie| ..
17. sūryagrahaṇa - sūryagrahaṇa hone para bhī kramaśaḥ āṭha, bāraha aura solaha prahara paryanta asvādhyāyakāla mānā gayā hai|
18. patana - kisī baḍa़e mānya rājā athavā rāṣṭrapuruṣa kā nidhana hone para jaba taka usakā dāhasaṃskāra na ho, taba taka svādhyāya nahīṃ karanā cāhie| athavā jaba taka dūsarā adhikārī sattārūḍha na ho, taba taka śanaiḥ śanaiḥ svādhyāya karanā cāhie|
19. rājavyudgraha - samīpastha rājāoṃ meṃ paraspara yuddha hone para jaba taka śānti na ho jāe, taba taka aura usake paścāt bhī eka dina-rātri svādhyāya nahīṃ kareṃ|
20. audārika śarīra - upāśraya ke bhītara paṃcendriya jīva kā vadha ho jāne para jaba taka kalevara paḍa़ā rahe, taba taka tathā 100 hātha taka yadi nirjīva kalevara paḍa़ā ho to svādhyāya nahīṃ karanā cāhiye|
asvādhyāya ke uparokta 10 kāraṇa audārikaśarīra sambandhī kahe gaye haiṃ|
21-28. cāra mahotsava aura cāra mahāpratipadā - āṣāḍha-pūrṇimā, āśvina-pūrṇimā, kārtikapūrṇimā aura caitra-pūrṇimā ye cāra mahotsava haiṃ| ina pūrṇimāoṃ ke paścāt āne vālī pratipadā ko mahāpratipadā kahate haiṃ| inameṃ svādhyāya karane kā niṣedha hai| .
29-32. prātaḥ, sāyaṃ, madhyāhna aura ardharātri - prātaḥ sūrya ugane se eka ghaḍa़ī pahile tathā eka ghaḍa़ī pīche| sūryāsta hone se eka ghaḍa़ī pahale tathā eka ghaḍa़ī pīche| madhyāhna arthāt dopahara meṃ eka ghaḍa़ī āge aura eka ghaḍa़ī pīche evaṃ ardharātri meṃ bhī eka ghaḍa़ī āge tathā eka ghaḍa़ī pīche svādhyāya nahīṃ karanā cāhie|

________________ āgama prakāśana samiti, byāvara dvārā prakāśita āgama-sūtra nāma anuvādaka-sampādaka ācārāṃgasūtra [do bhāga śrī candra surānā 'kamala' upāsakadaśāṃgasūtra ḍaॉ. chaganalāla śāstrī (ema. e. pī-eca. ḍī.) jñātādharmakathāṃgasūtra paṃ. śobhācandra bhārilla ankṛddaśāṃgasūtra sādhvī divyaprabhā (ema. e., pī-eca. ḍī) anuttarovavāiyasūtra sādhvī muktiprabhā (ema. e., pī-eca. ḍī) sthānāṃgasūtra paṃ. hīrālāla śāstrī samavāyāṃgasūtra paṃ. hīrālāla śāstrī sūtrakṛtāṃgasūtra śrī candra surānā 'surāṇā' vipākasūtra anu. paṃ. rośanalāla śāstrī sampā, paṃ. śobhācanda bhārilla nandīsūtra anu. sādhvī umarāvakuṃvara 'arcanā' sampā. kamalā jaina 'jījī' ema. e. aupapātikasūtra ḍaॉ. chaganalāla śāstrī vyākhyāprajñaptisūtra [cāra bhāga] śrī amaramuni rājapraśnīyasūtra vāṇībhūṣaṇa ratanamuni, saṃ. devakumāra jaina prajñāpanāsūtra [tīna bhāga] jainabhūṣaṇa jñānamuni praśnavyākaraṇasūtra anu. muni pravīṇaṛṣi sampā, paṃ. śobhācandra bhārilla uttarādhyayanasūtra śrī rājendramuni śāstrī nirayāvalikāsūtra śrī devakumāra jaina daśavaikālikasūtra mahāsatī puṣpavatī āvaśyakasūtra mahāsatī suprabhā (ema. e. pīeca. ḍī.) jambūdvīpaprajñaptisūtra ḍaॉ. chaganalāla śāstrī anuyogadvārasūtra upādhyāya śrī kevalamuni, saṃ. devakumāra jaina, sūryaprajñapti, candraprajñaptisūtra sampā. muni śrī kanhaiyālālajī 'kamala' jīvājīvābhigamasūtra [do bhāga] śrī rājendra muni niśīthasūtra muniśrī kanhaiyālāla jī 'kamala', śrī tilokamuni trīṇichedasūtrāṇi muniśrī kanhaiyālāla jī 'kamala', śrī tilokamuni śrī kalpasūtra (patrākāra) upādhyāya muni śrī pyāracaṃda jī mahārāja śrī antakṛddaśāṃgasūtra (patrākāra) upādhyāya muni śrī pyāracaṃdajī mahārāja viśeṣa jānakārī ke liye samparkasūtra āgama prakāśana samiti śrī braja-madhukara smṛti bhavana, pīpaliyā bājāra, byāvara-305901 di ḍāyamaṇḍa jubilī presa, ajamera 9431898
